==== FUNCTION 080491cf classify ====
PROTO int classify(int v)
CCONV unknown
---- DECOMP ----

/* WARNING: Function: __x86.get_pc_thunk.ax replaced with injection: get_pc_thunk_ax */
/* WARNING: Unknown calling convention */

int classify(int v)

{
  if (v == 7) {
    return 0x6b;
  }
  if (v < 8) {
    if (v == 6) {
      return 0x6a;
    }
    if (v < 7) {
      if (v == 5) {
        return 0x69;
      }
      if (v < 6) {
        if (v == 4) {
          return 0x68;
        }
        if (v < 5) {
          if (v == 3) {
            return 0x67;
          }
          if (v < 4) {
            if (v == 2) {
              return 0x66;
            }
            if (v < 3) {
              if (v == 0) {
                return 100;
              }
              if (v == 1) {
                return 0x65;
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return -1;
}

==== FUNCTION 0804930e munge ====
PROTO void munge(point * p, int k)
CCONV unknown
---- DECOMP ----

/* WARNING: Function: __x86.get_pc_thunk.ax replaced with injection: get_pc_thunk_ax */
/* WARNING: Unknown calling convention */

void munge(point *p, int k)

{
  int i;
  
  for (i = 0; i < k; i = i + 1) {
    p->x = p->x + i;
    p->y = p->y ^ p->x * 2;
  }
  return;
}
